# 编译 vivo-blueos

- **URL**: https://github.com/orgs/KMiri-rs/discussions/124
- **Category**: General
- **Author**: zjp-CN
- **Created**: 2026-08-01T14:47:48Z
- **Updated**: 2026-09-01T05:57:23Z
- **Comments**: 2

## Body

Get started: https://github.com/vivoblueos/book/blob/main/src/getting-started.md


# 拉取所有仓库

https://github.com/vivoblueos/manifests

需要安装 `ninja`、 `gn`、`repo` 工具，然后创建 `vivo-blueos` 目录，在该目录中运行：

```bash
repo init -u git@github.com:vivoblueos/manifests.git -b main -m manifest.xml && repo sync
```


```
./vivo-blueos/
├── apps
├── book
├── build
├── BUILD.gn -> build/toplevel/BUILD.gn
├── external
├── kernel
├── libc
├── librs
└── out
```


# Docker

Dev docker image (built by myself): https://hub.docker.com/repository/docker/zjpzjp/blueos

```yml
# vivo-blueos/docker-compose.yml
services:
  vivo-blueos:
    container_name: blueos-kmiri
    image: zjpzjp/blueos:latest
    network_mode: host
    command: sleep infinity
    volumes:
      - /home/zjp/KMiri/vivo-blueos:/blueos-dev
```

```bash
KMiri/vivo-blueos $ docker compose up -d
KMiri/vivo-blueos $ docker compose exec vivo-blueos bash
```

# 查看编译命令

```bash

$ ninja -C out/qemu_riscv64.debug -t commands blueos | rg rustc
```

```json
  "body": "Get started: https://github.com/vivoblueos/book/blob/main/src/getting-started.md\r\n\r\n\r\n# 拉取所有仓库\r\n\r\nhttps://github.com/vivoblueos/manifests\r\n\r\n需要安装 `ninja`、 `gn`、`repo` 工具，然后创建 `vivo-blueos` 目录，在该目录中运行：\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nrepo init -u git@github.com:vivoblueos/manifests.git -b main -m manifest.xml && repo sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n\r\n```\r\n./vivo-blueos/\r\n├── apps\r\n├── book\r\n├── build\r\n├── BUILD.gn -> build/toplevel/BUILD.gn\r\n├── external\r\n├── kernel\r\n├── libc\r\n├── librs\r\n└── out\r\n```\r\n\r\n\r\n# Docker\r\n\r\nDev docker image (built by myself): https://hub.docker.com/repository/docker/zjpzjp/blueos\r\n\r\n```yml\r\n# vivo-blueos/docker-compose.yml\r\nservices:\r\n  vivo-blueos:\r\n    container_name: blueos-kmiri\r\n    image: zjpzjp/blueos:latest\r\n    network_mode: host\r\n    command: sleep infinity\r\n    volumes:\r\n      - /home/zjp/KMiri/vivo-blueos:/blueos-dev\r\n```\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nKMiri/vivo-blueos $ docker compose up -d\r\nKMiri/vivo-blueos $ docker compose exec vivo-blueos bash\r\n```\r\n\r\n# 查看编译命令\r\n\r\n```bash\r\n$ gn gen out/qemu_riscv64.debug --args='board=\"qemu_riscv64\" build_type=\"debug\" direct_syscall_handler=false'\r\n$ ninja -C out/qemu_riscv64.debug -t commands blueos | rg rustc\r\nCARGO_PKG_VERSION=0.1.20 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_MAJOR=0 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_MINOR=1 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_PATCH=20 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_PRE= CARGO_PKG_NAME=semihosting CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR=. CARGO_CRATE_NAME=semihosting rustc --crate-name semihosting --crate-type bin --emit=dep-info=host/obj/external/vendor/semihosting-0.1.20/semihosting_semihosting_v0_1_build_script.d,link --cap-lints=allow --cfg=feature=\\\"alloc\\\" --cfg=feature=\\\"fs\\\" --cfg=feature=\\\"stdio\\\" --edition=2021 -Cmetadata=semihosting-0.1 -Copt-level=1 -Cforce-frame-pointers=yes -Cdebug-assertions -Cdebuginfo=full --color=always -D warnings -A unused -A dead_code -A static_mut_refs -o host/bin/semihosting_semihosting_v0_1_build_script ../../external/vendor/semihosting-0.1.20/build.rs   -Clink-arg=-Wl,-Map=host/obj/external/vendor/semihosting-0.1.20/semihosting_semihosting_v0_1_build_script.map && llvm-objcopy -O binary host/bin/semihosting_semihosting_v0_1_build_script host/bin/semihosting_semihosting_v0_1_build_script.bin  && echo 'CARGO_PKG_VERSION=0.1.20 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_MAJOR=0 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_MINOR=1 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_PATCH=20 CARGO_PKG_VERSION_PRE= CARGO_PKG_NAME=semihosting CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR=.
```
